# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-11-03)

## Corpus Check
- 4891 files · ~10,977,825 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 114 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 34, .css 17, .entitlements 11)

## Summary
- 63988 nodes · 186434 edges · 1713 communities (1037 shown, 676 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6283 edges (avg confidence: 0.85)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `8fe8b6b3`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
